1→/**
     2→ * 后台主布局：左侧可折叠菜单 + 顶栏（面包屑 / 搜索 / 通知 / 用户菜单）
     3→ */
     4→import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
     5→import { Link, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
     6→import { Breadcrumb, Layout, Menu, Spin } from 'antd'
     7→import { MenuFoldOutlined, MenuUnfoldOutlined, ShopOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons'
     8→import { useIsFetching } from '@tanstack/react-query'
     9→import { useAuthStore } from '@/store/auth'
    10→import { filterMenu, routeTitles } from './menu'
    11→import GlobalSearch from './GlobalSearch'
    12→import NotificationBell from './NotificationBell'
    13→import UserMenu from './UserMenu'
    14→
    15→const { Header, Sider, Content } = Layout
    16→
    17→/** 全局加载遮罩：存在进行中的请求时展示（延迟 250ms 避免闪烁） */
    18→function useGlobalLoading(): boolean {
    19→  const fetching = useIsFetching()
    20→  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false)
    21→
    22→  useEffect(() => {
    23→    if (fetching === 0) {
    24→      setVisible(false)
    25→      return
    26→    }
    27→    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setVisible(true), 250)
    28→    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
    29→  }, [fetching])
    30→
    31→  return visible
    32→}
    33→
    34→/** 主布局 */
    35→export default function MainLayout() {
    36→  const location = useLocation()
    37→  const permissions = useAuthStore((state) => state.permissions)
    38→  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false)
    39→  const globalLoading = useGlobalLoading()
    40→
    41→  const menuItems = useMemo(
    42→    () =>
    43→      filterMenu(permissions).map((group) => ({
    44→        key: group.key,
    45→        label: group.label,
    46→        icon: group.icon,
    47→        children: (group.children ?? []).map((item) => ({
    48→          key: item.key,
    49→          icon: item.icon,
    50→          label: <Link to={item.key}>{item.label}</Link>,
    51→        })),
    52→      })),
    53→    [permissions],
    54→  )
    55→
    56→  // 依据当前路径展开对应菜单分组
    57→  const openKeys = useMemo(() => {
    58→    for (const group of filterMenu(permissions)) {
    59→      if ((group.children ?? []).some((item) => item.key === location.pathname)) {
    60→        return [group.key]
    61→      }
    62→    }
    63→    return []
    64→  }, [location.pathname, permissions])
    65→
    66→  const selectedKeys = [location.pathname]
    67→  const currentTitle = routeTitles[location.pathname] ?? '后台管理'
    68→
    69→  return (
    70→    <Layout className="app-layout">
    71→      <Sider
    72→        theme="light"
    73→        collapsible
    74→        collapsed={collapsed}
    75→        trigger={null}
    76→        width={220}
    77→        collapsedWidth={64}
    78→        className="app-sider"
    79→      >
    80→        <div className="app-logo">
    81→          <ShopOutlined className="app-logo__icon" />
    82→          {!collapsed ? <span className="app-logo__text">ShopStack 中台</span> : null}
    83→        </div>
    84→        <Menu
    85→          mode="inline"
    86→          items={menuItems}
    87→          selectedKeys={selectedKeys}
    88→          defaultOpenKeys={openKeys}
    89→          key={openKeys.join(',')}
    90→        />
    91→      </Sider>
    92→
    93→      <Layout>
    94→        <Header className="app-header">
    95→          <div className="app-header__left">
    96→            <button
    97→              type="button"
    98→              className="app-collapse"
    99→              onClick={() => setCollapsed((prev) => !prev)}
   100→              aria-label="切换菜单折叠"
   101→            >
   102→              {collapsed ? <MenuUnfoldOutlined /> : <MenuFoldOutlined />}
   103→            </button>
   104→            <Breadcrumb
   105→              items={[{ title: 'ShopStack 中台' }, { title: currentTitle }]}
   106→              className="app-breadcrumb"
   107→            />
   108→          </div>
   109→          <div className="app-header__right">
   110→            <GlobalSearch />
   111→            <NotificationBell />
   112→            <UserMenu />
   113→          </div>
   114→        </Header>
   115→
   116→        <Content className="app-content">
   117→          {globalLoading ? (
   118→            <div className="app-loading-mask">
   119→              <Spin size="large" tip="加载中..." />
   120→            </div>
   121→          ) : null}
   122→          <Outlet />
   123→        </Content>
   124→      </Layout>
   125→    </Layout>
   126→  )
   127→}
```

    65→          ),
    66→        })),
    67→    }))
    68→  }, [permissionsQuery.data])
    69→
    70→  const refresh = () => {
    71→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['roles'] })
    72→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['permissions'] })

    74→
    75→  const openCreate = () => {

   198→              <Button type="link" size="small" danger icon={<DeleteOutlined />}>

   204→      ),
   205→    },
   206→  ]
   207→

   211→
   212→      <Card>

   214→          title="角色列表"

   219→                刷新
   220→              </Button>
   221→              {canManage ? (
   222→                <Button type="primary" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={openCreate}>
   223→                  新增角色
   224→                </Button>
   225→              ) : null}
   226→            </>
   227→          }
   228→        />
   229→        <Table<Role>
   230→          rowKey="id"
   231→          columns={columns}
   232→          dataSource={rolesQuery.data?.roles ?? []}
   233→          loading={rolesQuery.isLoading || rolesQuery.isFetching}
   234→          scroll={{ x: 1180 }}
   235→          locale={{ emptyText: '暂无角色' }}
   236→          pagination={false}
   237→        />
   238→      </Card>
   239→
   240→      <Drawer
   241→        title={editing ? `编辑角色 · ${editing.name}` : '新增角色'}
   242→        width={560}
   243→        open={drawerOpen}
   244→        onClose={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}
   245→        destroyOnHidden
   246→        extra={
   247→          <Space>
   248→            <Button onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
   249→            <Button type="primary" loading={submitting} onClick={handleSubmit}>
   250→              保存
   251→            </Button>
   252→          </Space>
   253→        }
   254→      >
   255→        <Form form={form} layout="vertical">
   256→          <Form.Item
   257→            label="角色名称"
   258→            name="name"
   259→            rules={[{ required: true, message: '请输入角色名称' }, { max: 20, message: '最多 20 个字符' }]}
   260→          >
   261→            <Input placeholder="如：运营专员" />
   262→          </Form.Item>
   263→          <Form.Item
   264→            label="角色编码"
   265→            name="code"
   266→            rules={[
   267→              { required: true, message: '请输入角色编码' },
   268→              { pattern: /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,30}$/, message: '仅支持小写字母、数字与下划线' },
   269→            ]}
   270→          >
   271→            <Input placeholder="如：operator" disabled={!!editing} />
   272→          </Form.Item>
   273→          <Form.Item label="角色描述" name="description">
   274→            <Input.TextArea rows={2} maxLength={100} showCount placeholder="角色职责说明" />
   275→          </Form.Item>
   276→          <Form.Item
   277→            label="权限配置"
   278→            name="permission_ids"
   279→            rules={[{ required: true, message: '请至少选择一个权限' }]}
   280→          >
   281→            <Tree
   282→              checkable
   283→              selectable={false}
   284→              treeData={permissionTree}
   285→              checkedKeys={checkedKeys}
   286→              onCheck={(keys) => {
   287→                const list = Array.isArray(keys) ? keys : keys.checked
   288→                setCheckedKeys(list as number[])
   289→                form.setFieldValue('permission_ids', list as number[])
   290→              }}
   291→            />
   292→          </Form.Item>
   293→        </Form>
   294→      </Drawer>
   295→    </PageContainer>
   296→  )
   297→}
```

### Shared component 1: `src/components/ResourceTable.tsx`

Props (config-driven):
```ts
export interface ResourceColumn<T> {
  title: string
  dataIndex: string
  width?: number
  align?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right'
  render?: (value: any, record: T, index: number) => ReactNode
  ellipsis?: boolean
}

export interface ResourceField {
  name: string
  label: string
  type?: 'text' | 'textarea' | 'number' | 'money' | 'switch' | 'select' | 'tags' | 'datetime'
  required?: boolean
  placeholder?: string
  options?: Array<{ label: string; value: number | string }>
  span?: number
  min?: number
  max?: number
  help?: string
  /** 列表页是否作为筛选项 */
  searchable?: boolean
  initialValue?: unknown
}

export interface ResourceListResponse<T> {
  items: T[]
  meta: PageMeta
}

export interface ResourceTableProps<T> {
  title: string
  description?: string
  /** 列表请求 */
  fetcher: (params: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<ResourceListResponse<T>>
  /** 表格列 */
  columns: ResourceColumn<T>[]
  /** 表单字段（新增/编辑） */
  fields?: ResourceField[]
  /** 保存 */
  onSave?: (values: Record<string, unknown>, editing: T | null) => Promise<unknown>
  /** 删除 */
  onDelete?: (record: T) => Promise<unknown>
  /** 列表项 key */
  rowKey?: string
  /** 额外的行操作 */
  rowActions?: (record: T) => ReactNode
  /** 额外工具条按钮 */
  toolbarExtra?: ReactNode
  /** 初始筛选 */
  initialParams?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** 空态文案 */
  emptyText?: string
  /** 字段值 → 表单初始值 */
  toFormValues?: (record: T) => Record<string, unknown>
  /** 列表接口 key（react-query） */
  queryKey?: string
}
```
